Method: Coda 让冻结的策略先完成少步数的噪声到动作轨迹；随后用一个轻量 Transformer，基于候选动作、源噪声和共享观测前缀缓存，预测由示范监督的残差。训练时仅训练校正器。

Result: 在 50 个 RoboTwin Easy 任务上，五步 Coda 将成功率从 71.64% 提升至 74.68%，相比默认十步策略前向延迟降低 30.2%；两步配置达到 71.88% 成功率并实现 2.12 倍加速。独立 13 任务对照在近乎相同延迟下提升 5.69 个百分点。该设计也改进了冻结的官方 SmolVLA，将两步成功率从 60.8% 提升至 69.4%。

Conclusion: 端点校正可有效替代额外积分步骤，改善冻结 flow-matching 策略的质量—延迟权衡。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies based on flow matching generate action chunks through repeated evaluations of an action expert. Increasing the number of integration steps raises inference cost, but does not necessarily improve closed-loop success. We propose Coda, which reallocates part of this integration budget to a single learned endpoint correction. A frozen policy first completes a few-step noise-to-action trajectory; a lightweight Transformer then predicts a demonstration-supervised residual using the candidate action, source noise, and shared observation-prefix cache. Only the corrector is trained. On 50 RoboTwin Easy tasks, five-step Coda improves success from 71.64% to 74.68% over the matched five-step baseline, while reducing forward latency by 30.2% relative to the default ten-step policy. A two-step configuration achieves 71.88% success with a 2.12$\times$ speedup. An independent 13-task control shows a 5.69-percentage-point gain at nearly equal latency, supporting correction as an effective alternative to additional integration. The same design also improves frozen official SmolVLA, raising two-step success from 60.8% to 69.4%. These results show that endpoint correction improves the quality-latency trade-off of frozen flow-matching policies.

</details>


### [107] [Safe Real-Time Policy Steering via Noise-Space Trajectory Optimization for One-Step Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21220)
*Qingyi Chen,Joseph Ruan,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: INSPO 将一步生成策略的推理时引导转化为策略输入噪声空间中的轨迹优化，通过在线粒子优化搜索行为空间，在不修改生成动作的前提下满足碰撞避免、姿态维持等约束，性能优于基线且运行更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器人策略能表达多模态行为，但预训练策略在部署时需适配碰撞避免、姿态维持等约束。现有推理时引导方法依赖扩散或流过程的迭代梯度引导，实时控制下计算开销大。

Method: 将一步生成策略的推理时引导建模为策略输入噪声空间中的轨迹优化：优化输入噪声并在诱导的状态轨迹上评估约束，从而在策略诱导的行为空间中搜索而不直接改动生成动作；引入正则项使解保持与策略输入分布一致，并用基于种群的粒子优化在线求解。

Result: 在 Push-T、Can pick-and-place 和 LIBERO-Spatial 上，对基于状态和图像的任务专用策略以及通用视觉-语言-动作策略进行评估。INSPO 在任务成功率和约束满足度上优于 best-of-N 采样与动作投影，并在更低运行时下与梯度引导生成方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 在噪声空间做轨迹优化是一步生成策略推理时引导的高效方案，兼顾实时性与约束满足，适用于任务专用及通用 VLA 策略。

Abstract: Generative robot policies can represent diverse, multimodal behaviors, but adapting pretrained policies to deployment-time constraints such as collision avoidance and orientation maintenance remains challenging. Existing inference-time steering methods typically apply gradient guidance through iterative diffusion or flow processes, which can be computationally expensive for real-time control. We propose INSPO, which formulates inference-time steering of one-step generative policies as trajectory optimization in the policy's input noise space. By optimizing the input noise while evaluating constraints on the induced state trajectory, INSPO searches the policy-induced behavior space without directly modifying generated actions. The optimization includes a regularization term that encourages solutions to remain consistent with the policy's input distribution and is solved online using population-based particle optimization. We evaluate INSPO on state- and image-based task-specific policies and generalist vision-language-action policies across Push-T, Can pick-and-place, and LIBERO-Spatial. INSPO improves task success and constraint satisfaction over best-of-N sampling and action projection, while comparing favorably with gradient-guided generation at lower runtime.

</details>


### [108] [SafeStage: Evaluating Safety Before, During, and After Vision-Language-Conditioned Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21223)
*Jinzhu Luo,Qi Zhang,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SafeStage——一个按"执行前/执行中/执行后"三阶段划分的机器人操作安全评测基准，含97个风险场景，将任务成功与安全性分开报告，揭示名义成功常与安全违规并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言条件机器人策略的评测多聚焦任务成功率、单一物理约束、语义拒答或已发生的物理损伤，难以定位闭环操作中"安全在何时失效"。作者希望建立一个能按时序阶段定位安全违规、并把任务成功与安全性解耦的诊断性评测体系。

Method: 构建SafeStage基准：97个专门设计的风险场景，分为三个阶段——初始状态隐患（操作目标前需解决的与安全相关的关系）、执行期安全（执行中的不安全接触、轨迹、区域进入与物体交互）、最终状态隐患（任务名义完成后残留的不稳定或不安全状态）。采用基于事件与基于状态的检查评估实际发生的交互，并在统一的闭环协议下评测代表性的直接动作VLA策略与基于世界模型的策略，任务成功率与各阶段安全结果独立报告。

Result: 评测显示：名义任务完成经常与安全违规同时出现；不同策略在三个阶段上表现出各自不同的失效特征（failure profiles）。

Conclusion: 通过将任务成功与安全性分离并定位违规发生的阶段，SafeStage为评估和改进视觉-语言条件机器人操作策略提供了统一的诊断性测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language-conditioned robot policies integrate perception, language understanding, and control for general-purpose manipulation. However, existing evaluations often focus on task success, isolated physical constraints, semantic refusal, or realized physical damage, providing limited insight into where safety fails during closed-loop manipulation. We introduce SafeStage, a lifecycle-structured benchmark for evaluating manipulation safety before, during, and after task execution. SafeStage contains 97 purpose-built risk scenarios organized into three stages. Initial-State Hazards captures safety-relevant relations that must be resolved before manipulating the target. Execution-Time Safety evaluates unsafe contacts, trajectories, region entries, and object interactions during execution. Final-State Hazards capture unstable or otherwise unsafe conditions remaining after nominal task completion. The benchmark evaluates realized interactions using event-based and state-based checks and reports native task success independently from stage-specific safety outcomes. We evaluate representative direct-action Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies and policies with world-model-based policies under a common closed-loop protocol. Our results demonstrate that nominal task completion frequently coexists with safety violations and that different policies exhibit distinct failure profiles across the three stages. By separating task success from safety and localizing when violations occur, SafeStage provides a unified diagnostic testbed for evaluating and improving vision-language-conditioned robot manipulation policies.

</details>


### [109] [AirSplan: Risk-Aware Motion Planning for Quadrotors in Cluttered 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21226)
*Seth Isaacson,William Hong,Katherine A. Skinner,Ram Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AirSplan 结合归一化 3D Gaussian Splatting 场景表示与基于可达性的运动规划器，为四旋翼在复杂场景中生成连续时间无碰撞路径，在挑战性测试中成功率达 81.2%，显著优于基线 51.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼广泛应用于农业、基础设施巡检与维护等场景，必须在复杂几何环境中严格无碰撞飞行。与地面机器人不同，空中机器人即使轻微碰撞也可能导致机体损毁。这一安全需求带来两个挑战：一是无真值障碍数据时仍需高保真表达复杂场景结构；二是规划器需利用该表示找到无碰撞路径。本文正是针对这两个互补挑战提出系统方案。

Method: AirSplan 采用 3D Gaussian Splatting 的归一化变体来编码高保真场景几何；并设计了一种新颖的基于可达性的运动规划器，利用四旋翼的微分平坦特性计算连续时间碰撞约束，从而对机器人占据空间进行紧致过近似。

Result: 实验表明，AirSplan 在 81.2% 的挑战性测试用例中成功找到路径，相比最接近的基线方法的 51.2% 有显著提升。

Conclusion: AirSplan 通过高保真场景表示与基于可达性的连续时间碰撞约束规划，有效解决了复杂环境中四旋翼的无碰撞飞行问题，成功率大幅领先现有基线方法。

Abstract: Quadrotors are increasingly deployed in applications such as agriculture, infrastructure inspection, and maintenance. In each of these applications, the robot must navigate complex scene geometry while remaining strictly collision-free. Unlike in ground domains, even minor collisions for aerial vehicles can result in the loss of the robot. This safety requirement induces a pair of technical challenges. First, the environment must be represented with sufficient fidelity to encode complex structure, even when no ground-truth obstacle data is available. Second, a motion planner must leverage this representation to determine a collision-free path to the goal. This paper proposes a system that addresses these complementary challenges. The proposed method, AirSplan, adopts a normalized variant of 3D Gaussian Splatting that encodes high-fidelity scene geometry. It then applies a novel reachability-based motion planner that leverages the differential flatness of quadrotors to compute continuous-time collision constraints that tightly overapproximate the robot's occupancy. Experiments demonstrate that AirSplan successfully finds a path in 81.2% of challenging test cases, a significant improvement over the nearest baseline method's 51.2%.

</details>


### [110] [FOCAL-VLA: Subtask-Guided Geometry Distillation and Implicit World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21228)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Di Wen,Yanxiang Zhan,Mohammad Khoshnazar,Jeroen Schäfer,Kunyu Peng,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FOCAL-VLA通过子任务引导的几何蒸馏与隐式世界建模，让VLA模型聚焦当前交互相关的几何与动力学表征，在仿真和真实操作任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预训练视觉语言模型的VLA模型直接将当前2D观测映射为动作，缺乏足够的空间与时间理解，难以完成精确和长时序操作。现有增强方法虽引入几何监督和全场景未来状态预测，但全场景信息冗余会干扰模型学习与当前交互相关的几何和动力学。

Method: 提出FOCAL-VLA框架，结合子任务引导的几何蒸馏与隐式世界建模。一方面从VGGT将几何知识迁移到VLA模型，通过将几何隐变量与子任务相关图像区域特征对齐，使几何学习聚焦当前子任务；另一方面利用当前与未来示范帧的Track4World特征进行隐式世界建模，捕捉当前交互的未来3D演化。两种互补表征联合引导动作生成，且推理时无需运行VGGT或Track4World。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实世界操作任务上，FOCAL-VLA均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 聚焦于当前子任务相关的几何与未来交互动力学表征，可有效缓解冗余场景信息干扰，提升VLA模型在精确和长时序操作中的表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models built on pretrained vision-language models have demonstrated strong performance across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. However, VLA models that directly map current 2D observations to actions often lack sufficient spatial and temporal understanding, limiting their performance in precise and long-horizon manipulation. Recent methods enhance VLA models through geometric supervision and future-state prediction across the entire scene. However, these methods can suffer from redundant scene information, distracting the model from learning the geometry and dynamics relevant to the current interaction. To address this issue, we propose FOCAL-VLA, a framework that combines subtask-guided geometry distillation with implicit world modeling to learn representations of current spatial structure and future interaction dynamics. To focus geometric learning on the current subtask, we transfer geometric knowledge from VGGT to the VLA model by aligning geometry latents with features from subtask-relevant image regions. To capture the future 3D evolution of the current interaction, we incorporate implicit world modeling using Track4World features from current and future demonstration frames. The two complementary representations jointly guide action generation without running VGGT or Track4World at inference time. Experiments show that FOCAL-VLA outperforms baselines on both simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks. Project website: https://zhiyuan-gao.github.io/FOCAL-VLA/.

</details>


### [111] [KnowDemo: Knowledge-Guided Robot Demonstration Generation from Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21229)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Yanxiang Zhan,Mohammad Khoshnazar,Jeroen Schäfer,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KnowDemo框架，利用人类视频中的结构化操作知识生成多样化机器人演示，提升行为多样性与生成效率，并实现三任务的仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人上采集演示数据成本高昂。现有从人类视频生成机器人演示的方法以"动作参考适配"为核心，一方面保留了原演示的接触策略与子任务顺序，限制了行为多样性；另一方面对任务需求与场景关系理解不足，会生成大量无效候选，降低生成效率。

Method: 基于视觉语言模型（VLM）构建知识提取与推理模块，将物体与动作描述同推断出的任务条件、演示参考及允许的执行变化关联起来，从而区分"任务需求"与"演示特定选择"；再将知识解析到目标场景的实体与几何上，指导候选生成与筛选，最后经运动规划与仿真验证。生成的演示包含替代接触策略、有效子任务顺序及结构化执行标签。

Result: 相比仅使用参考的配置，方法产生了更多经过验证的执行模式；通过任务引导的抓取采样提高了候选规划成功率；在仿真数据上微调预训练的π_{0.5}模型，在三个任务上实现了仿真到现实的迁移。

Conclusion: 以结构化操作知识驱动的演示生成可同时提升行为多样性与生成效率，且所生成数据适用于策略学习与真实机器人迁移。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation policies typically requires substantial demonstration data, which are costly to collect on real robots. Recent methods generate robot demonstrations from human videos by adapting recovered motion and validating the resulting trajectories in simulation. However, methods centered on motion-reference adaptation can limit behavioral diversity by retaining the demonstrated contact strategies and subtask orders, while insufficient understanding of task requirements and scene relations can reduce demonstration generation efficiency by generating invalid candidates. To address these limitations, we propose KnowDemo, a framework that uses structured manipulation knowledge from human videos to generate diverse robot demonstrations for a target workspace. To distinguish task requirements from demonstration-specific choices, we develop a knowledge extraction and reasoning module based on a vision-language model (VLM) that associates object and action descriptions with inferred task conditions, demonstration references, and permissible execution variations. To translate this knowledge into executable demonstrations, we resolve the descriptions against target-scene entities and geometry to guide candidate generation and screening before motion planning and simulation. The resulting demonstrations exhibit multimodal behavior through alternative contact strategies and valid subtask orders, with structured execution labels. Experiments demonstrate additional verified execution modes beyond a reference-only configuration and improved candidate planning success through task-guided grasp sampling. To validate the generated data for policy learning, we fine-tune the pretrained $π_{0.5}$ model on simulation data, achieving sim-to-real transfer across three tasks. Project page: https://zhiyuan-gao.github.io/knowdemo/

</details>


### [112] [VLA-Scope: Shift-Aware Failure Prediction for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21246)
*Kaiwen Zhu,Dongfang Liu,Liangkai Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLA-Scope两阶段框架，结合输入偏移表征与执行历史，在OOD条件下预测VLA模型执行失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将视觉观测与语言指令映射为机器人动作，但分布偏移会损害其可靠性；且在OOD条件下模型仍可能成功，因此仅检测OOD输入不足以预测执行失败。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段用池化图像与语言表征检测OOD输入并分类偏移类别；第二阶段对被标记为OOD的输入，结合预测类别、动作前缀特征与执行进度特征，用一个跨类别共享的逻辑回归模型随执行推进更新失败风险。

Result: 在OpenVLA与十个LIBERO-Spatial任务上采用留一组交叉验证：OOD检测ROC-AUC为0.9454，偏移分类准确率91%；在全部1400条OOD rollout上独立评估失败预测器，执行60步后ROC-AUC为0.8497，而不含执行进度特征时为0.7906，且高于ActProbe与SAFE-MLP基线。

Conclusion: 将动作特征与时间聚合的执行步表征结合，可提升输入偏移下的执行失败预测能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models map visual observations and natural-language instructions to robotic actions, but distribution shifts can compromise their reliability. Because these models may still succeed under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions, detecting OOD inputs alone is insufficient to predict execution failure. In this paper, we introduce VLA-Scope, a two-stage framework that combines input-shift characterization with execution history to predict failure during OOD rollouts. The first stage uses pooled image and language representations to detect OOD inputs and classify their shift categories. For inputs flagged as OOD, the second stage combines the predicted category, action-prefix features, and execution progress features. A logistic regression model shared across shift categories updates failure risk as execution proceeds. We evaluate the framework with OpenVLA on ten LIBERO-Spatial tasks using leave-one-group-out cross-validation. OOD detection achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.9454, and shift classification achieves 91% accuracy. Evaluated independently of the OOD gate on all 1,400 OOD rollouts, the failure predictor achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.8497 after 60 executed actions, compared with 0.7906 without execution progress features. It also achieves a higher ROC-AUC than the evaluated ActProbe and SAFE-MLP baselines. These results suggest that combining action features with temporally aggregated execution step representations improves failure prediction under input shifts.

</details>


### [113] [LOInK: Learned Optimal Inverse Kinematics via Structured Neural Surrogate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21275)
*Michael Somerfield,Damian Abood,Ruigang Wang,Ian R. Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LOInK方法，通过学习从构型空间到解耦任务/潜空间的双Lipschitz可逆映射，将代价最小解置于潜空间原点，并借助基于算子分裂的网络反演算法高效生成近似最优逆运动学解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆运动学问题通常需要求解带约束的优化问题以得到代价最小解，计算开销大。现有生成式方法需大量采样并评估候选解，难以直接高效生成高质量的最优解。因此作者希望学习一种能直接、高效产生近似最优逆运动学解的表示与反演方法。

Method: LOInK在由采样构型、对应任务变量和给定代价函数组成的数据上训练，学习一个从构型空间到解耦的任务/潜空间的双Lipschitz可逆映射，并将代价最小解构造在潜空间原点处。随后利用基于算子分裂（operator splitting）的网络反演算法，在潜空间中高效采样代价最小的解，再映射回构型空间。

Result: 作者在三个问题上验证了方法：一个用于说明的三自由度机械臂问题；一个四足攀爬机器人问题，其中LOInK生成近优解平均比约束优化方法快31倍、最多快100倍；以及一个纯数据驱动的仿真软体驱动器示例，LOInK能显式生成高质量解，而现有生成式方法需要多样化采样与候选解评估。

Conclusion: LOInK通过可逆映射与潜空间原点结构化，实现了近似最优逆运动学解的高效生成，在多个任务上显著优于约束优化与现有生成式方法，兼具速度与解质量优势，并适用于纯数据驱动场景。

Abstract: We introduce Learned Optimal Inverse Kinematics (LOInK), a method to generate approximately optimal solutions to an inverse kinematics problem. When trained on data consisting of sampled configurations and associated task variables and a given cost function, LOInK learns a bi-Lipschitz invertible mapping from configuration space to a decoupled task/latent space, and moreover, the latent space is structured so as to place cost-minimizing solutions at the origin. This enables efficient sampling of cost-minimizing solutions via a network-inversion algorithm based on operator splitting. We demonstrate the proposed approach on three problems: an illustrative three degree-of-freedom manipulator problem; a quadrupedal climbing robot for which LOInK can generate near-optimal solutions on average 31 times faster and up to 100 times faster than a constrained optimization approach; and a simulated soft actuator as a purely data-driven example, in which LOInK can explicitly generate high-quality solutions, unlike existing generative approaches that require diverse sampling and evaluation of candidate solutions.

</details>


### [114] [Stability-aware Residual Reinforcement Learning Framework for Robotic Manipulator Disturbance Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21307)
*Jihong Kim,Joonhyuk Kwon,Hwa Soo Kim,TaeWon Seo,Hyung-Tae Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出残差强化学习扰动观测器（DOB）框架，将解析观测器与RL策略结合，用RL补偿模型无法捕捉的残差，并通过ISS分析施加状态相关动作约束以保证稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制器与扰动观测器在机械臂精密跟踪中受参数不确定性、非线性摩擦和复合扰动影响，性能受限。

Method: 采用解析观测器作为确定性基线在可靠区域工作，RL策略专门补偿残差；引入估计器网络将观测历史与特权扰动上下文对齐，按扰动状态组织潜空间以快速适应扰动切换；基于输入-状态稳定性（ISS）分析推导并施加状态相关的动作边界。

Result: 在6自由度机械臂上，扰动估计与跟踪性能均有提升；零样本仿真到现实迁移下真实硬件跟踪误差降低27.8%，在训练中未出现的基座振动扰动下降低38.0%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过解析观测器与RL残差补偿的结合，在保证闭环跟踪误差受限的前提下，实现了对未知扰动的鲁棒精密跟踪。

Abstract: Although conventional controllers and disturbance observers (DOBs) are the standard for precision tracking in manipulators, they suffer from parameter uncertainty, nonlinear friction, and compound disturbances. This study proposes a residual reinforcement learning DOB framework that pairs an analytical observer with an RL policy. The deterministic baseline operates within a reliable region, whereas the RL policy explicitly targets the residuals that the model cannot capture. To make this compensation disturbance-aware, an estimator network aligns the observation history with a privileged disturbance context, organizing the latent space by disturbance regime and enabling rapid adaptation across disturbance transitions. To guarantee stability, we derived and enforced a state-dependent action bound on the RL policy from an input-to-state stability (ISS) analysis such that the closed loop provably confines the tracking error to a certified envelope for arbitrary policy outputs. Experiments on a 6-DOF manipulator demonstrated consistent improvements in disturbance estimation and tracking, including a 27.8% tracking-error reduction on real hardware under zero-shot sim-to-real transfer and a 38.0% reduction under a base-vibration disturbance that was not observed during training.

</details>


### [115] [NaViRrator: Robot Navigation from Human-Readable Maps through a Learned Visual Route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21316)
*Ayun Lee,Jiseon Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NaViRRator框架，将人类可读地图上的起终点转化为导航指令，通过RouteScribe分解为路线推理与语言生成两步，实验证明其在成功率和SPL上优于多个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可读地图虽提供了直观的机器人目标指定接口，但将其示意几何与机器人自我中心观测相结合仍具挑战性。核心动机是构建一个模块化接口，使人类可读地图与预训练导航策略能够有效衔接，并支持执行器的独立替换。

Method: 核心方法为RouteScribe，将路线推理与语言表达分离：首先在地图图像坐标中生成显式路线骨架（route scaffold），再由预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）将其转化为导航指令。骨架通过起终点直线条件流匹配（SGL-CFM）构建，将直线起终点路点序列变形为地图条件路线。执行阶段，VLN策略仅接收指令和自我中心观测，地图与骨架保留在上游，因此无需重新训练地图到语言的模块即可替换执行器。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，相比直接地图到指令生成、基于A*的骨架生成和高斯源条件流匹配，本方法在成功率和SPL上均更高。定性结果显示其能产生更清晰的显著转向，并更好地保留预期的动作序列。

Conclusion: 以路线为基的语言可作为人类可读地图与预训练导航策略之间的模块化接口，验证了路线推理与语言表达分离设计的有效性。

Abstract: Human-readable maps provide an intuitive interface for specifying robot destinations, but connecting their schematic geometry to egocentric observations remains challenging. We present NaViRRator, a framework that translates user-specified start and goal locations on such maps into navigation instructions for a pretrained vision-and-language navigation (VLN) policy. Its core method, RouteScribe, separates route inference from verbalization by first generating an explicit route scaffold in map-image coordinates, which a pretrained vision-language model (VLM) converts into a navigation instruction. We construct the scaffold with start--goal line conditional flow matching (SGL-CFM), which deforms a straight start--goal waypoint sequence into a map-conditioned route. During execution, the VLN policy receives only the instruction and egocentric observations, while the map and scaffold remain upstream, allowing executor replacement without retraining the map-to-language modules. Real-world experiments show higher success rates and success weighted by path length (SPL) than direct map-to-instruction generation, A*-based scaffolding, and Gaussian-source conditional flow matching. Qualitative results further show clearer salient turns and better preservation of the intended maneuver sequence, supporting route-grounded language as a modular interface between human-readable maps and pretrained navigation policies.

</details>


### [116] [LEMCA: LLM-Guided Synthesis of Efficient Mode-Switching Control Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21319)
*Arjun Krishna,Vincent Pacelli,Dinesh Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出LEMCA，用大语言模型引导合成高效的"模式切换控制"架构，自动发现能在"简单"任务阶段回收浪费资源、超越单体控制器Pareto前沿的控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界的物理控制任务（如驾驶、物体操作）在感知与计算复杂度上会随时间剧烈变化，因此按资源分配动态切换控制模式是更节省资源的自然选择。但"模式切换控制器"历来需要针对每个新任务费力地进行专家式设计与综合，导致现代机器人控制架构常退回到"单体式"一刀切结构，资源分配被永久锚定在最困难、最耗资源的任务阶段，造成浪费。

Method: 提出LEMCA，将模式切换控制器设计表示为可解释的程序，并在进化循环中迭代精化。为评估设计适应度，提出面向模式切换控制器的自动控制器综合扩展方法（如仿真中的强化学习），再借助大语言模型的语义先验、推理与编码能力，迭代编辑控制器模式、其对应的感知-计算资源分配以及模式切换。

Result: 在多种控制基准上，LEMCA一致地发现能够通过在"简单"任务阶段回收浪费资源而超越单体设计Pareto前沿的策略。

Conclusion: LEMCA提供了一条自动化、低成本的途径来合成资源高效的模式切换控制器设计。

Abstract: Physical control tasks in the natural world, such as driving or object manipulation, frequently exhibit dramatic variations in sensory and compute complexity over time. Correspondingly, a natural resource-efficient choice for robot control is to dynamically switch between control modes with varying resource allocations. However, such "mode-switching controllers" (MSCs) have historically required laborious, expert-driven design and synthesis for each new task. Driven by these design difficulties, modern robotic control architectures often fall back to a wasteful "monolithic" one-size-fits-all structure, where resource allocation is permanently anchored to the hardest, most resource-intensive task phases. To facilitate the design of performant yet efficient MSCs, we propose LLM-Guided synthesis of Efficient Mode-Switching Control Architectures (LEMCA). LEMCA represents MSC designs as interpretable programs to be iteratively refined in an evolutionary loop. To evaluate design fitness, we propose MSC-compatible extensions of automated controller synthesis approaches, such as reinforcement learning in simulation. LEMCA then leverages the semantic priors, reasoning, and coding capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to iteratively edit controller modes, their corresponding sensory-compute resource allocations, and mode transitions. Our experiments across diverse control benchmarks show that LEMCA consistently discovers strategies that surpass the Pareto frontier of monolithic designs by reclaiming wasted resources during "easy" task phases. LEMCA thus presents an automated, low-effort path to synthesize resource-efficient MSC designs.

</details>


### [117] [The EventCV Library for Event-Based Robotic Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21330)
*Adam D. Hines,Michael Milford,Tobias Fischer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EventCV 是一个开源可扩展的 Rust 库（带 OpenCV 风格 Python 绑定），统一集成事件相机的驱动、去噪、几何变换、增强、角点检测、无监督特征学习、运动估计、视频转事件仿真和 ONNX 推理，并在 Jetson Orin AGX 上完成三项机器人案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级异步响应、高动态范围和低功耗等优势，适合快速运动或复杂光照下的机器人，但实际集成仍面临缺少即插即用驱动、事件流文件格式互不兼容、依赖定制化科研级代码等障碍，入门门槛高。

Method: 采用 Rust 实现核心库并提供 OpenCV 风格的 Python 绑定；功能涵盖去噪滤波、几何变换、数据增强、角点检测、无监督特征学习、对比度最大化运动估计、视频转事件仿真以及 ONNX 模型推理；集成 Neuromorphic Drivers 包以支持实时事件流处理。

Result: 在表示构建与文件解码上比现有库快 1.1x 至 3.7x；在 Jetson Orin AGX 上部署，并展示了目标检测、端侧模型推理和定位三项机器人案例；目前没有其他工具包能在单一包内覆盖如此广泛的操作。

Conclusion: EventCV 通过开源、可扩展且功能集成的设计降低了使用事件相机的研究与工程门槛，可支持实时机器人流水线部署。项目网页：https://eventcv.net。

Abstract: Event cameras detect per-pixel brightness changes asynchronously on microsecond timescales, with high dynamic range and low power draw. These are desirable properties for robots that move fast or work in difficult lighting conditions. However, integrating an event camera into a real-world robotic pipeline still requires substantial effort: plug-and-play drivers do not exist, event streams are recorded in a variety of incompatible file formats, and most projects rely on custom research-grade code. Here, we present EventCV, an open-source and extensible Rust library with OpenCV-style Python bindings that lowers the entry barrier to working with event cameras. EventCV provides a wide range of features: denoising filters and geometric transforms, augmentations, corner detection and unsupervised feature learning, contrast-maximization motion estimation, a video-to-events simulator, and Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) inference for deployment in robotic stacks. EventCV integrates the Neuromorphic Drivers package, allowing an event camera stream to be processed directly in real time. No existing toolkit covers this range of operations in one package, and EventCV builds representations and decodes files 1.1x to 3.7x faster than the currently available libraries. We deploy EventCV on a Jetson Orin AGX and present three robotics case studies spanning object detection, on-device model inference, and localization. Project webpage: https://eventcv.net.

</details>


### [118] [FAN: Foresight Action Normalization for Continual Adaptation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21358)
*Yijun Hong,Jiarun Zhu,Xiaoquan Sun,Le Xu,Qijun He,Xin Jin,Mingqi Yuan,Wenjun Zeng,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究持续VLA适应中的动作归一化问题，提出FAN方法，通过一次性校准并冻结归一化统计量，在四个真实任务流上取得最高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练于大规模闭集数据的VLA模型在多种机器人操作任务上表现优异，但长期真实部署需在获取新技能的同时保留旧能力。现有持续VLA适应方法（如经验回放、强化微调）忽视了动作归一化这一基础机制，而它决定了策略感知与执行物理动作的底层坐标系。

Method: 系统评估五种归一化策略，覆盖单臂与双臂操作的四个真实任务流；分析其失效模式（任务间坐标漂移、运动覆盖不足、训练-测试坐标不匹配）；据此提出一致性、覆盖性、因果性（3C）三条设计原则，并引入前瞻动作归一化（FAN）：在持续学习前从一个小规模、任务无关的校准集中一次性估计归一化统计量，并在整个适应过程中冻结。

Result: 在所有评估任务流中，FAN取得最高性能，并展现出持续的鲁棒性，优于现有归一化协议。

Conclusion: 动作归一化是持续VLA适应的关键但被忽视的机制；遵循3C原则的FAN为构建稳定动作表示、实现有效的终身VLA适应提供了指导。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models pre-trained on large-scale, closed datasets have demonstrated remarkable success across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. However, their long-term real-world deployment necessitates continuously acquiring new skills while retaining previously learned capabilities. While pioneering works have explored continual VLA adaptation using techniques such as experience replay and reinforcement fine-tuning, they overlook a foundational mechanism: action normalization, which determines the underlying coordinate system in which policies perceive and execute physical actions. To bridge this gap, we systematically evaluate five normalization strategies across four real-world task streams covering single-arm and bimanual manipulation. Our analysis reveals that existing protocols induce severe failure modes due to inter-task coordinate drift, limited motion coverage, or train-test coordinate mismatches. Motivated by these insights, we formulate three core design principles: consistency, coverage, and causality (3C), and introduce foresight action normalization (FAN). FAN estimates normalization statistics once from a small, task-independent calibration set prior to continual learning and freezes them throughout adaptation. Across all evaluated streams, FAN achieves the highest performance and demonstrates consistent robustness, providing insightful guidance for building stable action representations in achieving effective lifelong VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [119] [MicroHookACT: Monocular Microscopic Vision Guided Visuomotor Policy for Flexible Microelectrode Hooking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21365)
*Yitong Chen,Fangbo Qin,Yang Wang,Ruihua Hu,Kui Zhang,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroHookACT，一种基于模仿学习的视觉运动策略，用于单目显微视觉下的自动化3D微针-微环挂钩操作，成功率96.7%，平均耗时11.5秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性微电极（FME）植入中，自动化针-环挂钩是关键步骤，但在单目显微视觉下实现微米级精确对齐与接触式穿线仍具挑战。

Method: 包含三个核心设计：1）单向挂钩策略，利用散焦线索与光轴引导实现免触诊的精确对齐与富接触穿线；2）基于冻结ViT主干的动作监督物体注意力模块，直接从人类示范中学习聚焦微针尖与微环，无需人工视觉标注；3）以注意力为中心的全局粗特征与局部细特征，依据预测动作进度动态加权，使单一ACT策略适应操作中变化的散焦模糊与视觉需求。

Result: 在60个人类示范上训练视觉运动策略，在5种不同难度设置下评估，MicroHookACT取得最高96.7%的总体成功率，平均执行时间11.5秒。

Conclusion: 结果验证了视觉运动策略学习在多变操作条件下实现微米级控制的潜力。

Abstract: Automated needle-loop hooking is a critical step in flexible microelectrode (FME) implantation. This paper presents MicroHookACT, an imitation learning-based visuomotor policy for automated 3D hooking under monocular microscopic vision. First, a unidirectional hooking strategy exploits defocus cues and optical-axis guidance to enable palpation-free precise alignment and contact-rich threading. Second, an action-supervised object attention module built on a frozen ViT backbone learns to focus on the micro-needle tip and micro-loop directly from human demonstrations, without requiring manual visual annotations for training. Third, attention-centered global coarse and local fine features are dynamically weighted according to predicted action progress, enabling a single ACT policy to adapt to changing defocus blur and visual requirements throughout the operation. In the experiments, visuomotor policies were trained on 60 human demonstrations and evaluated under five setups with varying difficulties. Our MicroHookACT framework achieved the highest overall success rate of 96.7\% with an average execution time of 11.5 s. These results demonstrate the potential of visuomotor policy learning for micron-level control under varying operating conditions.

</details>


### [120] [ProTracer: Proprioception-Guided Failure Diagnosis in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21369)
*Chang Dong,Mehdi Hosseinzadeh,King Hang Wong,Lingqiao Liu,Francois Fraysse,Feras Dayoub,Minh Hoai Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ProTracer框架，无需训练，利用VLM与本体检出信号实现机器人操作失败的检测、分类、解释及时序起点定位，并构建FailTime基准验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作失败分析多聚焦于失败检测与分类，缺少对失败发生时序起点的精细定位，难以为后续纠错提供精确时间信息。

Method: ProTracer为免训练框架：利用本体检出信号的动力学特征识别具有时序信息的动作边界，并将机器人状态信号转化为结构化自然语言描述，与视觉观测一起交由VLM联合推理，融合本体的时序精度与VLM的多模态推理能力。

Result: 实验表明ProTracer在传统失败诊断任务（二分类检测、分类、解释）以及新提出的失败起点定位任务上均取得良好性能，验证了本体检出推理对细粒度时序失败分析的重要性。

Conclusion: 将本体检出信号与VLM结合可在无需额外训练的情况下提升机器人失败分析能力，失败起点定位这一新任务及FailTime基准为细粒度时序失败分析提供了评估基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive framework for robot manipulation failure analysis that includes binary failure detection, failure categorization, explanation generation, and the additional capability of failure onset localization, which aims to identify the earliest moment at which a robot execution deviates from a valid task-completion trajectory and is ultimately followed by task failure. To address these tasks, we propose ProTracer, a training-free framework that leverages existing Vision-Language Models (VLMs) together with proprioceptive signals for failure analysis. Our method uses proprioceptive dynamics to identify temporally informative action boundaries and converts richer robot-state signals into structured natural-language descriptions that can be jointly analyzed together with visual observations by the VLM. This design combines the temporal precision of proprioceptive signals with the multimodal reasoning capabilities of modern VLMs without requiring additional model training. We further introduce FailTime, a benchmark with synchronized visual and proprioceptive observations for evaluating conventional failure diagnosis tasks as well as failure onset localization. Experiments demonstrate that ProTracer achieves strong performance across both conventional failure diagnosis tasks and the newly introduced failure onset localization task, highlighting the importance of proprioceptive reasoning for fine-grained temporal failure analysis.

</details>


### [121] [AVT-Fabric: Active Visuo-Tactile Perception via Adaptive Evidence Selection for Efficient Robotic Fabric Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21377)
*Chang Gao,Zhuo Chen,Suhang Xia,Jihong Zhu,Jiankang Deng,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AVT-Fabric是一种RGB优先的自适应视触觉织物比较框架，按需分配触觉证据，在400组对比中以7B模型达98.0%准确率，兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人织物比较需要结合视觉外观与触觉线索，但全阶段触觉采集成本高、延迟大，如何在保证准确率的同时高效选择触觉证据是关键问题。

Method: 提出AVT-Fabric框架：采用双尺度门控，基于答案token置信度与原始logit分离度判断是否需要额外的带力标记GelSight触觉观测；用紧凑文本记忆保存已执行历史，并通过多数投票整合选出的预测结果。

Result: 在400组留出对比上，7B MLLM达到98.0%准确率，超过90B MLLM-Fabric基线的94.0%（+4.0个百分点），平均仅使用5个阶段中的1.60个；相比匹配的被动推理提升9.25个百分点，模型侧延迟降低61.8%，并优于纯RGB准确率。另有4个MLLM骨干验证了泛化性、准确性与效率；实机部署实现78.1%成对排序准确率，8个场景中7个正确选材。

Conclusion: AVT-Fabric表明自适应证据选择能同时提升机器人视触觉推理的准确率与效率。

Abstract: Robotic fabric comparison needs to actively combine visual appearance and tactile cues. Here, we present AVT-Fabric, an RGB-first framework that allocates tactile evidence according to the difficulty of each comparison. A dual-scale gate evaluates answer-token confidence and raw logit separation to determine whether another force-tagged GelSight observation is needed. Compact textual memory preserves the executed history, and majority voting consolidates the selected predictions. On 400 held-out comparisons, AVT-Fabric achieves 98.0% accuracy with a compact 7B Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM), surpassing the 94.0% reported by the 90B MLLM-Fabric baseline by 4.0 percentage points while processing only 1.60 of five available stages on average. It improves on matched passive inference by 9.25 percentage points and reduces model-side latency by 61.8%, while also improving on RGB-only accuracy. Four additional MLLM backbones support the generalizability, accuracy, and efficiency of the framework. This framework is also deployed on a real robotic system, achieving 78.1% pairwise ranking accuracy and correct fabric selection in seven of eight application scenarios. AVT-Fabric demonstrates that adaptive evidence selection can improve both the accuracy and efficiency of robotic visuo-tactile reasoning.

</details>


### [122] [MarineCraft: Enabling Rapid Prototyping of Underwater Robots via Modular Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21396)
*Yuta Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MarineCraft：一种通过结构重构加速水下机器人开发的模块化工具包，采用自含防水推进模块，实现无集中布线的快速组装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人开发受防水和布线复杂性的制约，显著拖慢了快速原型迭代过程。

Method: 设计自含式防水推进模块，集成电源、无线通信与驱动，消除集中布线并免去反复密封，使多种机器人几何构型可在数分钟内完成组装并测试。

Result: 实验表明该可重构架构支持快速迭代设计周期，并在最深2.5米深度下保持可靠运行与无泄漏性能。

Conclusion: 该工具包将模块化转化为快速物理原型的手段，有效降低了水下机器人研发的门槛。

Abstract: Underwater robot development is often hindered by the complexities of waterproofing and wiring, which significantly delay the rapid prototyping process. This paper presents MarineCraft, a modular toolkit designed to accelerate the development cycle through structural reconfiguration. The system features self-contained, waterproof propulsion modules that integrate power, wireless communication, and actuation. By eliminating centralized wiring and the need for repeated sealing, MarineCraft allows diverse robot geometries to be assembled and tested in minutes rather than days. Experimental results demonstrate that this reconfigurable architecture enables fast, iterative design cycles while maintaining reliable operation and leak-free performance at depths of up to 2.5 meters. Our toolkit effectively lowers the barrier to underwater robotics by transforming modularity into a vehicle for rapid physical prototyping.

</details>


### [123] [Stabilizing Trajectory Outputs in End-to-End Autonomous Driving via SC-IMM Based Teacher Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21404)
*Siewoo Kim,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于场景条件交互多模型（SC-IMM）的离线教师信号生成与学习方法，通过在训练中引入路径-速度教师标签和模式后验概率作为辅助监督，在不改变推理结构与控制器的情况下提升端到端自动驾驶轨迹输出的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于路点的模仿学习主要最小化坐标级误差，难以捕捉随场景变化的路点路径-速度变化以及路点输出在时间上的不稳定性，导致闭环驾驶行为抖动和不稳定。

Method: 提出SC-IMM方法：将专家轨迹转换为路径-速度状态，并以场景线索为条件进行IMM更新，生成路径-速度教师标签与模式后验概率；将这些信号作为辅助监督加入原始轨迹损失中用于训练，而推理结构和路点控制器保持不变。

Result: 在CARLA Town12的100条短路线闭环评测中，相比基线驾驶得分提升28.0%，Collision/km降低62.3%，同时提升了jerk和轨迹变化相关指标。

Conclusion: 嵌入场景条件运动模型线索的离线教师信号能够引导轨迹输出型驾驶模型实现更稳定的闭环行为。

Abstract: End-to-End autonomous driving models commonly predict future waypoints from sensor inputs and convert them into vehicle control commands through a downstream controller. However, conventional waypoint-based imitation learning mainly minimizes coordinate-level errors, making it difficult to capture scene-dependent path-speed changes and temporal instability across waypoint outputs. In this paper, we propose an offline teacher-signal generation and learning method for trajectory-output stabilization based on a Scene-Conditioned Interacting Multiple Model (SC-IMM) to mitigate this issue. The proposed method converts expert trajectories into path-speed states and performs IMM updates conditioned on scene cues to generate path-speed teacher labels and mode posterior probabilities. The generated signals are added to the original trajectory loss as auxiliary supervision during training, while the inference structure and waypoint controller remain unchanged. In closed-loop evaluation on 100 short routes in CARLA Town12, the proposed method improved the driving score by 28.0% and reduced Collision/km by 62.3% compared with the baseline, while also improving jerk and trajectory-variation metrics. These results demonstrate that offline teacher signals embedding scene-conditioned motion-model cues can guide trajectory-output driving models toward more stable closed-loop behavior.

</details>


### [124] [A Unified Dynamic Force Guidance Framework for Performance-Optimized Kinesthetic Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21416)
*Chunxin Li,Jianhua Wu,Zhenhua Xiong,Xiangyang Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合性能约束与优化机制的在线动态力引导方法，用于协作机器人示教，以提升机器人的可操作性能并进而提高工作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人常用于产品频繁换型的工业场景，手把手示教（kinesthetic teaching）能实现快速部署。但用户在示教过程中容易忽视机器人的构型配置，导致可操作性能下降，影响后续作业效率。因此需要一种方法来实时约束并优化示教过程中的机器人性能。

Method: 提出在线动态力引导方法，集成性能约束与优化机制。其中，变导纳控制（variable admittance control）保证机器人可操作性能维持在预设阈值之上；虚拟力（virtual force）主动引导用户将机器人拖动至性能更优的构型。可操作性能以雅可比矩阵的最小奇异值（Minimum Singular Value）进行量化。

Result: 在6自由度协作机器人上开展实验，比较任务空间中的三条典型路径。通过轨迹回放实验评估示教轨迹质量，分析了机器人可操作性能与工作效率之间的关系。结果表明，所提方法有效提升了机器人的可操作性能，并相应提高了工作效率。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效提升协作机器人示教过程中的可操作性能，从而提高工作效率，对工业部署中缩短生产节拍时间具有重要价值。

Abstract: Collaborative robots are increasingly deployed in industrial scenarios characterized by frequent product changeovers. As an intuitive programming method, kinesthetic teaching facilitates rapid robot deployment. However, users may overlook the configuration of the robot during kinesthetic teaching, leading to degradation in operational performance. Operational performance refers to the capability of the robot to generate motion and can be quantified by the Minimum Singular Value of the Jacobian matrix. To address this issue, this paper proposes an online dynamic force guidance method that integrates performance constraint and optimization mechanisms. Specifically, variable admittance control maintains the operational performance of the robot above a predefined threshold, while a virtual force actively guides the user to drag the robot towards configurations with improved performance. Experiments are conducted on a 6-DOF collaborative robot, comparing three typical paths in the task space. To evaluate the quality of the taught trajectories, trajectory playback experiments are conducted to analyze the relationship between the operational performance of the robot and the work efficiency. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively enhances the operational performance of the robot and consequently improves the work efficiency, holding significant value for reducing production takt time in industrial deployment.

</details>


### [125] [FootQuery: Future-Touchdown-Guided Retrieval from Depth History for Perceptive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21447)
*Tao Dong,Jia Yu,Yuxuan Fan,Linna Zhao,Jiaqi Gong,Andong Yang,Chao Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FootQuery：以每只脚预测的下一落点来查询深度历史帧的感知式人形运动框架，实现复杂地形连续行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂地形下落脚点常到触地时已被遮挡或超出相机视野，仅靠当前观测不足，需从更早的观测中检索相关地形信息。

Method: 策略从本体感知预测落点位置及不确定性，结合单脚特征，对稀疏采样的历史深度帧进行查询式检索；训练时将实际接触投影到历史图像中，监督接触可见区域的检索；检索到的单脚特征与全局视觉记忆融合生成控制动作。训练采用渐进式力辅助课程和事件一致的踏面中线塑形。

Result: 仿真中，完整框架在最难的楼梯、间隙与平台场景上优于各组件消融；在Unitree G1实机上，单一策略完成室外楼梯及室内含上下楼梯、平台与间隙的路线连续通行。

Conclusion: 按预期接触来组织视觉历史，是感知式人形运动的一种有效方式。部署仅需本体感知与机载深度图像。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion over complex terrain requires anticipating footholds that may no longer be visible at touchdown. Limited camera coverage and self-occlusion make it necessary to retrieve relevant terrain information from earlier observations. We present FootQuery, a perceptive locomotion framework that queries depth history using each foot's predicted next touchdown. The policy predicts touchdown locations and uncertainty from proprioception and uses these distributions, together with per-foot features, to query sparsely sampled historical depth frames. During training, realized contacts are projected into historical images to supervise retrieval at the regions where those contacts were visible. The retrieved per-foot features are fused with global visual memory to generate control actions. A progressive force-assistance curriculum supports early exploration, while event-consistent tread-midline shaping encourages coordinated stair contacts. Deployment requires only proprioception and onboard depth images. In simulation, the complete framework outperforms its component ablations on the most challenging tested stairs, gaps, and platforms. Real-world experiments on a Unitree G1 demonstrate continuous traversal with a single policy across outdoor stairs and indoor routes combining stair ascent and descent, platforms, and gaps. These results support organizing visual history around anticipated contacts for perceptive humanoid locomotion.

</details>


### [126] [Robotic Multiphase Interaction: Manipulating Coupled Liquid and Solid Dynamics with a World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21448)
*Yixuan Feng,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出机器人多相交互(RMI)新设定：液体渗入可变形多孔固体骨架并与之力学耦合，以水饱和海绵为首个案例，构建含隐式不可压多孔流与SPH的仿真环境，结合接触记忆、速度/力混合调控与提升稳定性门控，并训练动作条件世界模型与Diffusion Policy/整流流匹配动作生成器，显著降低持水量预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作研究多集中于纯固体对象，或虽同时涉及液体与固体但保持两相空间分离；而液体渗入可变形多孔骨架的耦合场景（如抓取吸水海绵、溢出液体）在家庭与制造环境中既常见又影响抓取稳定性与安全，却缺乏建模范式与操控方法。作者因此提出RMI设定以填补这一空白。

Method: 以含水海绵为RMI首个实例，采用隐式不可压多孔流模型与平滑粒子流体动力学(SPH)作为动力学引擎；为实现机器人操控加入库仑接触记忆、速度与力的混合调控，以及用于提升操作的稳定性门控；由此构建把机器人指令映射到液固耦合状态变化的环境。随后训练以动作为条件的世界模型预测候选指令下状态演化，并用时间UNet以Diffusion Policy或整流流匹配生成动作。

Result: 所提世界模型相比基线将持水量预测误差降低60%以上；在世界模型从策略提案中筛选出的最优动作序列作用下，预测的终端水误差进一步减少约一半。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对液体与固体耦合状态进行建模有助于机器人预测其动作如何同时影响多孔物体及其内部所持液体，为RMI这一新任务设定的操控与规划提供了可行途径。

Abstract: This work presents \textit{Robotic Multiphase Interaction (RMI)}, a setting in which liquid enters a porous material and interacts mechanically with its deforming solid skeleton. Manipulation can therefore change pore volume, expel or redistribute retained liquid, and alter grasp stability at the same time. Spilled liquid can also create safety risks in domestic and manufacturing settings. This differs from most manipulation of solid objects and from tasks that involve both liquid and solid while keeping the phases spatially separate. We study a sponge filled with water as the first RMI example. We use implicit incompressible porous flow with smoothed particle hydrodynamics as the dynamics engine and enable robotic manipulation by adding Coulomb contact memory, hybrid velocity and force regulation, and a stability gate for lifting. The resulting environment connects robot commands to changes in the coupled liquid and solid state. A world model conditioned on actions predicts how this state evolves under candidate commands, while a temporal UNet generates actions using either Diffusion Policy or rectified flow matching. Our world model reduces retained water prediction error by more than $60\%$ compared with the baseline. The best action sequence selected by the world model from policy proposals further reduces the predicted terminal water error by about half. These improvements show that modelling the coupled liquid and solid state helps the robot predict how its actions affect both the porous object and the liquid held inside.

</details>


### [127] [AtomEgo: Exploring Ego-Robot Integration for Embodied Foundation Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21461)
*Di Wu,Dongchen Zheng,Junhe Sheng,Zhongxing Wei,Songxin Zhang,Zejian Xie,Xiaoquan Sun,Junyang Zheng,Zhuoyang Song,Jiaxing Zhang,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AtomEgo，系统研究自我中心（ego）人类数据与机器人数据的联合预训练，基于约2659小时语料和可扩展数据处理流水线，揭示"数据规模×对齐质量→能力增益"的核心原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身基础模型受限于机器人演示数据的规模与多样性，因此需要引入大规模自我中心人类交互数据。但人类与机器人之间存在显著的具身差异和动作空间鸿沟，如何有效利用这类数据进行预训练尚不明确。

Method: 构建约2659小时的数据语料和可扩展数据处理流水线；在视觉-语言-动作与"世界-动作"两类模型架构上，研究三种代表性范式：带领域专用动作头的联合共训练、通过具身对齐的渐进式ego-to-robot迁移、以及视频-动作联合建模；并通过多任务真实机器人实验和语言条件化的跨具身表征分析进行评估。

Result: 实验揭示了"数据规模 × 对齐质量 → 能力增益"这一简洁原则：自我中心数据能够提升泛化能力，但其价值取决于对齐与利用的有效程度。

Conclusion: 该原则为可扩展的ego-robot预训练提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models are constrained by the limited scale and diversity of robot demonstrations, motivating the use of large-scale egocentric human interaction data. However, how to effectively incorporate such data into embodied-model pre-training remains unclear because of substantial embodiment and action-space gaps between humans and robots. We present AtomEgo, a systematic study of ego--robot co-training supported by a curated corpus of approximately 2,659 hours and a scalable data processing pipeline. Across vision--language--action and world--action model architectures, we investigate three representative paradigms: joint co-training with domain-specific action heads, progressive ego-to-robot transfer through embodiment alignment, and joint video--action modeling. We evaluate these paradigms through multi-task real-robot experiments and language-conditioned cross-embodiment representation analysis. Our results reveal a simple principle: Data Scale * Alignment Quality --> Capability Gain; egocentric data can improve generalization, but their value depends on how effectively they are aligned and utilized. This principle can provide practical guidance for scalable ego--robot pre-training.

</details>


### [128] [Learning Distance-Conditioned Object Transport for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from a Single Motion Clip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21467)
*Yuhyeon Hwang,Daniel Sungho Jung,YongHyeok Seo,Mingi Jung,Chang Nho Cho,Jung-Hoon Hwang,Dongin Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出距离条件参考重组（DCRR）方法，将示教运动中的终止片段重定位到中间运输状态，从而构建距离条件监督并蒸馏为无参考策略，实现可调节运输距离的类人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于单一重定向运动片段训练的策略通常只能复现示教中的运输结果。源轨迹虽经过中间物体位移状态，但仅在终点展示了运输终止，作者将此不匹配识别为"终止-经过差距"（termination-versus-passage gap）：中间位移仅作为经过状态被观测，而非终止完成的结果。

Method: 提出DCRR：将示教的终止片段重新定位到中间运输状态以生成重组参考；冻结的跟踪教师模型在闭环动力学下重放这些重组参考；保留下来的轨迹按实际达到的物体放置位置重新标注，并蒸馏为无参考策略。该流程从源运动中编码的交互行为构建出距离条件监督。

Result: 在Carry、Kick-Push、Crouch-Push和Drag四类任务中，DCRR-BC产生命令依赖的运输行为，整体归一化距离MAE为0.15，优于仅用源行为的模仿学习（0.28）。RL微调进一步提升了训练仿真器中的命令响应与执行鲁棒性，并在sim-to-sim迁移下保持效果。硬件实验验证了四种交互模式下的运输距离调节能力。

Conclusion: DCRR能够从单一示教运动中构建距离条件监督，使策略实现可命令调节的物体运输，并具备仿真与真机上的鲁棒泛化能力。

Abstract: Motion tracking can reproduce humanoid loco-manipulation from a single retargeted motion clip, but a policy trained on a fixed reference primarily reproduces its demonstrated transport outcome. Although the source trajectory visits intermediate object displacements, transport termination is demonstrated only at its endpoint. We identify this mismatch as the termination-versus-passage gap: intermediate displacements are observed as passage states rather than termination-complete outcomes. We introduce Distance-Conditioned Reference Recomposition (DCRR), which relocates the demonstrated termination segment to intermediate transport states. A frozen tracking teacher replays the recomposed references under closed-loop dynamics, and the retained trajectories are relabeled by their achieved object placements and distilled into a reference-free policy. This procedure constructs distance-conditioned supervision from the interaction behavior encoded in the source motion. Across Carry, Kick-Push, Crouch-Push, and Drag, DCRR-BC produces command-dependent transport with an overall normalized distance mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.15, compared with 0.28 for source-only behavior cloning. RL fine-tuning further improves the command response and execution robustness in the training simulator and under sim-to-sim transfer. Finally, hardware experiments demonstrate transport-distance modulation across all four interaction modes.

</details>


### [129] [Adaptive Rollout Truncation Based on Epistemic Uncertainty for Efficient Offline World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21482)
*Nikodem Sebastian Zymla,Laurin Thiele,Johannes Pitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于认知不确定性的自适应展开策略，用于离线世界模型训练，在保持或提升预测精度的同时大幅减少计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步自回归训练能提升长时程预测能力，但固定展开长度会增加计算成本，并在模型尚不准确时放大早期训练误差；现有训练方案在优化过程中通常使用固定展开长度，不依赖模型当前的预测可靠性。

Method: 提出以认知不确定性驱动的自适应展开策略，采用自动课程训练方式：当认知不确定性超过由预热阶段校准的阈值时终止自回归展开。研究了两种不确定性估计器：共享循环骨干的五头集成模型，以及蒙特卡洛 Dropout。并设计了包含预热阶段的两阶段流程以稳定不确定性估计，之后启用自适应截断。

Result: 在 ANYmal-D 和 ANT 上的实验表明，基于集成的自适应截断在预测精度上匹配或优于固定长度训练和 RWM-U 基线，同时所需累计展开步数显著更少。在 ANYmal-D 上按该方法训练世界模型达到与基线相当的最终性能，展开计算量约减少 72%。

Conclusion: 认知不确定性不仅可用于下游策略正则化，也可用于提升世界模型训练本身的计算效率。

Abstract: Accurate neural world models are central to model-based robotics, where they enable robots to predict future states from previously observed trajectories. Multi-step autoregressive training improves long-horizon prediction, but fixed rollout horizons also increase computational cost and can amplify early training errors when the model is still inaccurate. Existing training schemes typically use the same rollout length throughout optimization, independent of the model's current predictive reliability.
  We propose an epistemic uncertainty-driven adaptive rollout strategy for offline world model training following an auto-curriculum training scheme. Instead of always unrolling to a fixed horizon, the model terminates autoregressive rollouts once epistemic uncertainty exceeds a threshold calibrated from a warm-up phase. We study two uncertainty estimators: a five-head ensemble with a shared recurrent backbone and Monte Carlo Dropout. A two-stage warm-up procedure stabilizes uncertainty estimates before we enable adaptive truncation.
  Experiments on ANYmal-D and ANT show that ensemble-based adaptive truncation matches or improves the prediction accuracy of fixed-horizon training and the RWM-U baseline while requiring substantially fewer cumulative rollout steps. Training a world model on ANYmal-D following the presented approach reaches comparable final performance with the baselines with roughly 72% less rollout computation. These results indicate that epistemic uncertainty is useful not only for downstream policy regularization, but also for making world model training itself more compute-efficient.

</details>


### [130] [FORTE: Task-Adaptive Force Capability Optimization for Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21497)
*Xiao Wang,Heng Zhang,Gokhan Solak,Fei Zhao,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向任务的力能力优化框架，用于冗余移动机械臂，通过VLM推断物体物理属性并生成任务力需求，将任务力不确定球与剩余力多面体的有符号距离作为力能力指标，融入全身多目标轨迹优化，实现重载保力、轻载保灵巧的任务自适应平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冗余分解方法常忽略具体任务的力需求，或一味最大化力能力，导致在不需要大力度余量时牺牲了灵巧性。机器人物理交互既需运动学可行，也需足够的力交互能力，因此需要与任务相关的力能力评估与优化。

Method: 1) 用视觉语言模型(VLM)从RGB图像与任务描述推断物体物理属性，生成包含重力与惯性需求的目标任务力序列；2) 定义面向任务的力能力指标，即任务力不确定球与动态残余力多面体(RFP)之间的有符号距离，衡量任务需求与机器人剩余驱动能力的匹配度；3) 将该指标与可操作性、关节限位回避、轨迹平滑、基座振荡抑制一起，构成全身多目标轨迹优化问题。

Result: 在移动机械臂执行举升与单点保持任务、不同负载条件下的实验中，本方法对重载能提供足够力能力，对轻载保持高可操作性，实现任务自适应平衡；而固定能力最大化基线(RFP内切半径、RFP锥)与仅可操作性优化均无法达到该效果。

Conclusion: 面向任务的力能力指标能有效协调力能力与灵巧性，在冗余移动机械臂上实现随负载变化的自适应权衡。核心实现已开源。

Abstract: Effective physical interaction control in robotic manipulation requires not only kinematically feasible motion but also sufficient force-interaction capability. Existing redundancy resolution methods often ignore task-specific force demands or maximize the force capability indiscriminately, sacrificing dexterity when large force margins are unnecessary. We propose a task-oriented force capability optimization framework for redundant mobile manipulators. A Vision-Language Model (VLM) infers object physical properties from an RGB image and a task description, generating a desired task-force sequence that captures gravitational and inertial demands. We then define a task-oriented force capability metric as the signed distance between a task-force uncertainty ball and the dynamic residual force polytope (RFP), quantifying compatibility between task demands and the robot's remaining actuation capacity. This metric is incorporated, alongside manipulability, joint-limit avoidance, trajectory smoothness, and base-oscillation suppression, into a whole-body multi-objective trajectory-optimization problem. Experiments on a mobile manipulator performing lifting and single-point-holding tasks under varying payload conditions demonstrate that the proposed method provides sufficient force capability for heavy loads while preserving high manipulability for light loads. This yields a task-adaptive balance that fixed capability-maximizing baselines (RFP inscribed radius, RFP cone) and manipulability-only optimization fail to achieve. The core implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/yeying256/FORTE.

</details>


### [131] [DPed-VLN: A Benchmark for Socially Compliant Vision-and-Language Navigation in Dynamic Pedestrian Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21504)
*Haojie Dai,Xiangyi Wang,Liuyi Wang,Kai Sheng,Zongtao He,Chengju Liu,Wei Ye,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DPed-VLN，一个面向动态行人的VLN基准，配套DPet策略网络，实验表明LoRA微调可提升VLM基线表现，DPet-RL在成功率等指标上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN研究多集中于静态室内环境，而真实场景中的机器人需在有人群活动的空间中理解语言指令，同时应对移动行人并满足社会安全约束，缺乏相应基准与方法。

Method: 构建基于Habitat 3.0的DPed-VLN基准，包含33,093条导航回合、成对的全局指令与先验增强指令、ORCA控制的人形行人、社会约束专家路径及联合评估效率与安全性的指标；并设计DPet行人感知策略网络，采用强化学习与模仿学习联合训练；同时通过LoRA微调将NaVILA、StreamVLN等VLM导航模型适配到该基准。

Result: LoRA适配在多项成功率和安全性指标上提升了零样本VLM基线，尤其显著降低了StreamVLN的碰撞率；在所评估方法中，DPet-RL取得最高的SR、SPL和STL。

Conclusion: DPed-VLN为动态行人场景下的VLN提供了可控分析基准，验证了先验增强指令与行人感知策略的有效性，DPet-RL表现最佳，LoRA微调是提升现有VLM模型社会安全性的一条可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) has advanced rapidly in static indoor environments, but robots operating in human-populated spaces must ground language while responding to moving pedestrians and social-safety constraints. We present DPed-VLN, a Habitat 3.0 benchmark for dynamic-pedestrian VLN that couples 33,093 navigation episodes with paired global and prior-augmented instructions, ORCA-controlled humanoid pedestrians, socially constrained expert paths, and metrics that jointly assess navigation efficiency and social safety. DPed-VLN separates ordinary goal-oriented route guidance from prior-augmented instructions that expose dynamic-pedestrian cues for controlled analysis. To instantiate the benchmark, we introduce DPet (Dynamic Pedestrian-aware Network), a pedestrian-aware policy network trained with reinforcement learning and imitation learning. We further adapt representative state-of-the-art VLM-based navigation models, including NaVILA and StreamVLN, to DPed-VLN through LoRA fine-tuning. Experiments show that LoRA adaptation improves zero-shot VLM baselines in several success and safety metrics, especially reducing StreamVLN's collision rate. Among the evaluated methods, DPet-RL achieves the highest SR, SPL, and STL.

</details>


### [132] [2nd Place Solution to the HANDS 2026 Workshop Challenge-Dexterous Grasp Motion Track: Single-Shot Trajectory Warping for Grasp Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21511)
*Muneeb A. Khan,Woojin Kim,Shinwoo Kim,Muhammad Munsif,Binod Bhattarai,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"编辑单条演示"的抓取动作生成方法，由策略一次观测物体并输出12维warp，开环重放，用一步PPO并行训练，取得HANDS 2026挑战赛第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务要求为12自由度LinkerHand O6在随机初始手姿下生成物理可行的抓取-抬升轨迹。难点在于：每步需做约70次12维决策，误差会随时间累积；测试物体与物理动力学可能偏离训练分布。逐步生成策略难以稳定。

Method: 不逐步生成动作，而是编辑一条成功的GraspM3演示：策略只观测物体一次，输出一个12维的warp（演示形变），随后开环重放该形变后的轨迹。warp策略使用一步PPO在所有4,824个训练物体上并行训练。

Result: 在私有测试集上，简单赛道成功率94.61%（所有提交中最高），困难赛道成功率57.18%。

Conclusion: 将序列生成问题转化为单步预测+开环重放，可有效规避误差累积，并通过大规模并行一步PPO高效训练；该方案在挑战赛中获第二名，验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This report describes our 2nd place solution to the HANDS 2026 workshop challenge (Dexterous Grasp Motion track) in conjunction with ECCV 2026. In this challenge, we address grasp motion generation for the 12-DoF LinkerHand O6, aiming to produce physically plausible reach-and-lift trajectories for unseen objects from randomized initial hand poses in simulation. This task is particularly challenging because each grasp requires a per-step policy to make approximately $70$ twelve-dimensional decisions, with errors accumulating over time, while test objects and physical dynamics may differ from those encountered during training. To address these challenges, we propose editing a single successful GraspM3 demonstration instead of generating the motion step by step: a policy observes the object once and outputs a 12-D warp of the demonstration, which is then replayed open-loop. Moreover, we train the warp policy with one-step PPO over all $4{,}824$ training objects in parallel. As a result, our method achieved success rates of $94.61\%$ on the easy track, the highest of all submissions, and $57.18\%$ on the hard track of the private test set.

</details>


### [133] [Skel-WAM: A Hand-Skeleton-Conditioned World Action Model for Human-to-Robot Manipulation Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21514)
*Zetao Cai,Yaping Li,Yiqun Wang,Xinyu Zhan,Yuyin Yang,Haoxiang Ma,Kailin Li,Tao Lu,Jiangmiao Pang,Linning Xu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Skel-WAM 世界动作模型，通过统一手部骨架运动接口弥合人-机器人视觉与动作空间差距，实现联合学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示采集成本高、任务变化覆盖有限；人类视频是低成本补充操作经验，但存在视觉外观与动作空间的具身差距。

Method: 以共同手部拓扑对齐人类与机器人运动，结合骨架叠加（场景接地）与结构化 2.5-D 关键点（显式手部运动学）；视频专家与关键点专家通过 Mixture-of-Transformers 联合学习视觉与骨骼动态，独立的机器人训练动作专家将预测映射为可执行控制，从而无需机器人动作标签即可用人类视频监督共享动态。

Result: 在四个真实双臂任务和七个仿真任务上，平均成功率分别为 79.86% 和 63.29%，超越最强基线 22.22 和 8.28 个百分点；人-机器人协同训练在机器人训练数据未覆盖的任务变化上，将真实成功率从 38.89% 提升至 86.11%（提升一倍以上）。

Conclusion: 共享骨架接口可实现人类与机器人数据的联合学习，并借由互补的人类演示扩展机器人任务覆盖范围。

Abstract: Robot demonstrations are expensive to collect and often provide limited distributional coverage of task variations. Human videos offer a low-cost source of complementary manipulation experience, but learning from them requires bridging embodiment gaps in visual appearance and action spaces. We introduce Skel-WAM, a world action model that bridges these differences through a unified hand-skeleton motion interface. The key insight is to align human and robot motion through a common hand topology, combining skeleton overlays that ground motion in the scene with structured 2.5-D keypoints that encode explicit hand kinematics. Video and Keypoint Experts jointly learn visual and skeletal dynamics through a Mixture-of-Transformers, while a separate robot-trained Action Expert maps these predictions to executable controls. This separation enables human and robot demonstrations to directly supervise shared dynamics without requiring robot action labels for human videos. Across four real-world bimanual tasks and seven simulated tasks, Skel-WAM achieves average success rates of 79.86% and 63.29%, surpassing the strongest baseline by 22.22 and 8.28 percentage points, respectively. Human-robot cotraining more than doubles real-world success on task variations absent from robot training data, from 38.89% to 86.11%. These results demonstrate that a shared skeletal interface enables joint learning across human and robot data and expands robot task coverage through complementary human demonstrations.

</details>


### [134] [SABER: Learning Attention-based Semantic Affordance for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21572)
*Hari Prasanth Palanivelu,Samuel Sze,Kennard Garrison Johannes,Albertus Hendrawan Adiwahono,Meng Yee,Chuah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SABER，一种无规划器的强化学习策略，通过统一的"地形可供性地图"（几何+语义接触代价）与带符号语义偏置注意力机制，让四足机器人选择合法落脚点并避开禁止接触区域。移除语义偏置使违规接触增加55%，并在Unitree B2上完成室内外sim-to-real验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知式腿足运动虽已把地形几何融入学习策略，但对"地形含义"的利用仍很稀疏：管道、草地、易碎箱子等在几何上可通行却不适合踩踏。在工业场景中，一次错误落脚就可能损坏脆弱设备、使机器人失稳甚至危及现场安全。因此需要让策略同时理解几何可通行性与语义接触许可。

Method: SABER为无规划器的强化学习策略，联合推理地形几何与语义接触许可。输入为统一的地形可供性地图，每个格子编码局部3D几何与语义接触代价。在交叉注意力中引入可学习的带符号语义偏置：作为注意力logits上的加性项，由接触代价门控，并按距离最近足端的距离对标记单元重新加权——使危险在仍能影响下一个落脚点时重塑注意力，在无法影响时其作用衰减。策略据此在许可支撑面上选落脚点，并在整个摆动相保持腿部远离禁区。作者还做了系统消融以分离各架构组件的贡献。

Result: 消融实验显示，仅移除语义偏置就使违规（禁止）接触增加55%，而速度跟踪性能保持不变。策略在Unitree B2上验证，实现了室内外环境、四类语义障碍的sim-to-real语义接触选择。

Conclusion: 将语义接触许可与几何信息统一编码并注入注意力机制，可有效实现面向语义的落脚点选择，且不牺牲运动速度跟踪性能，具备从仿真到真实机器人的迁移能力。

Abstract: Perceptive legged locomotion has advanced rapidly by integrating terrain geometry into learned policies, yet the integration of terrain meaning remains sparse: a pipe, a patch of grass, or a fragile box may be geometrically traversable while being inappropriate for contact. In industrial environments, where legged robots increasingly operate, a single misplaced step can damage fragile equipment, destabilize the robot, or endanger the site. To address this, we introduce SABER, a planner-free reinforcement-learning policy that jointly reasons about terrain geometry and semantic contact permission. The policy consumes a unified terrain-affordance map, where each cell encodes local 3D geometry and a semantic contact cost. We augment cross-attention with a learned, signed semantic bias: an additive term on the attention logits, gated by the contact cost, that reweights flagged cells by their distance from the nearest foot. A hazard therefore reshapes attention where it can still affect the next foothold, and its influence fades where it cannot. The resulting policy selects footholds on permitted support and keeps the leg clear of forbidden regions throughout the swing phase. We perform a systematic ablation that isolates the contribution of each architectural component; removing the semantic bias alone increases forbidden contacts by 55% while velocity tracking is unchanged. We validate the policy on a Unitree B2, demonstrating sim-to-real semantic contact selection across indoor and outdoor environments and four semantic obstacle classes.

</details>


### [135] [Tilt as a Certified Resource: Preserving Motor Wrench-Rate Authority on Articulated Multirotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21580)
*Giuseppe Silano,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于主动形态学的配置相关"就绪度证书"，并作为控制障碍函数嵌入统一物理指令二次规划，解决全驱动多旋翼在扰动下权威保持的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典力分配器仅追踪标称力旋量，忽略快速调制能力的动态极限；最大就绪度会导致拓扑不连通的最优面，要求物理上不可能的驱动器速率；在固定几何对称平台上强制就绪度安全下限还面临零和退化：电机转速再分配无法在不牺牲力旋量追踪的前提下提升权威。

Method: 用主动形态学打破退化，将伺服倾转视为提供静态旋翼所不具备的权威恢复方向的几何资源。构建配置相关、仅含电机的就绪度证书——可达力旋量速率集的对数体积，显式排除伺服容量以避免"幽灵容量谬误"。将该证书作为控制障碍函数（CBF）在统一物理指令二次规划中约束电机力矩与伺服设定点。

Result: 在强阵风扰动下对铰接八旋翼进行闭环仿真：经典分配器发散，未认证的铰接分配器违反安全下限，而所提出的CBF滤波器能约束系统状态并保持飞行器权威。

Conclusion: 主动形态学结合电机专用就绪度CBF可在保持力旋量追踪的同时维持系统权威，避免经典与未认证分配器的失效模式。

Abstract: Fully-actuated multirotor aerial vehicles must not only track nominal wrenches but retain the "readiness" to modulate them rapidly under disturbances. Classical effort-minimizing allocators ignore this dynamic limit, whereas maximizing readiness leads to topologically disconnected optimal sheets demanding physically impossible actuator rates. Enforcing a readiness safety floor on fixed-geometry symmetric platforms further encounters a zero-sum degeneracy: motor-speed redistribution cannot improve authority without conceding wrench tracking. This paper uses active morphology to break the degeneracy, treating servo tilt as a geometric resource supplying authority-recovery directions unavailable to static rotors. We construct a configuration-dependent, motor-only readiness certificate - the log-volume of the reachable wrench-rate set - that explicitly excludes servo capacity, preventing a "ghost capacity fallacy" in which the certificate would falsely credit slow mechanical kinematic limits instead of collapsing accurately at motor saturation. The certificate is enforced as a Control Barrier Function (CBF) within a Unified Physical-Command Quadratic Program acting on motor torques and servo setpoints. Closed-loop simulations of an articulated octorotor under severe gust disturbances show classical allocators diverging and uncertified articulated allocators violating the safety floor, while the proposed CBF filter bounds the system state and preserves vehicle authority.

</details>


### [136] [A High-Payload Wall-Climbing Robot Using Passive Bistable Suction Cups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21584)
*Andrew Nguyen,Mingyuan Li,Daniel Bruder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种采用被动双稳态吸盘的高负载爬壁机器人，通过履带机构自动接合/脱开吸盘，实现光滑表面攀爬并具备较高负载能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爬壁机器人可自动化窗户清洗、检测、维护和建造等危险或劳动密集任务。主动粘附负载能力高但需持续供电维持抓附；被动粘附（如吸盘）无需供电，但负载能力受限，因此需要兼顾高负载与低功耗的方案。

Method: 设计一种新型高负载爬壁机器人，采用被动双稳态吸盘，无需被压向墙面即可产生粘附；并引入履带式系统，自动接合与脱开双稳态吸盘，实现光滑表面上的运动。

Result: 机器人可在玻璃、木材、金属和涂漆表面实现垂直爬壁，也可侧向和倒置攀爬，并能拖曳7.940 kg负载，负载自重比为2.25。

Conclusion: 被动双稳态吸盘结合履带式自动接合/脱开机制，可在无需持续供电的条件下实现高负载爬壁运动，验证了其在多种光滑表面上的攀爬与拖曳能力。

Abstract: Wall-climbing robots capable of scaling vertical surfaces could help automate hazardous or labor intensive tasks such as window washing, inspection, maintenance, and construction. Active adhesion methods achieve higher payload capacities, but require power to maintain their grip. Passive adhesion devices such as suction cups are an attractive option for such robots because they do not require power to maintain their grip, but they are limited by their payload capacity. This work presents a novel high-payload wall-climbing robot that utilizes passive bistable suction cups to generate adhesion without needing to be pushed into the wall. The robot features a track-based system that automatically engages and disengages bistable suction cups to achieve locomotion on smooth surfaces. The robot is able to achieve vertical wall climbing on glass, wood, metal, and painted surfaces, sideways and upside-down climbing, and is able to tow a payload of 7.940 kg (with a payload-to-weight ratio of 2.25).

</details>


### [137] [Potential-Field Action Representation for Reinforcement Learning in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21609)
*Xinyu Liu,Gökhan Solak,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出PA-RL框架，用人工势场作为强化学习的动作表示，策略仅调节势场参数而非直接输出运动指令，在插孔任务中达到100%仿真成功率并成功零微调迁移到真机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无模型强化学习虽能通过试错习得接触密集的操作技能，但策略需同时学习任务策略与底层运动生成；直接笛卡尔指令接口要求策略在每个决策步生成运动，将任务级适应与连续底层控制耦合，加重学习负担，因此动作表示的选择至关重要。

Method: 提出PA-RL：策略不直接命令运动，而是调整类能量人工势场的参数，由势场生成状态相关的引导方向，再通过笛卡尔阻抗控制器执行。在插孔装配这一代表性接触密集任务上，与笛卡尔速度、笛卡尔位姿、可变阻抗三种动作空间在相同RL算法下对比。

Result: PA-RL是唯一在限定训练时间内达到100%评估成功率的方法，最佳基线为92.6%；相较最佳基线，关节力矩变化降低55.4%，笛卡尔加速度变化降低70.8%，且奖励中未加入显式运动质量惩罚项。仿真训练的策略无需微调即完成9/9真机插孔。

Conclusion: 以人工势场作为动作表示能有效减轻策略学习负担，兼顾任务成功率与运动质量，并展现出仿真到真机部署的可行性。

Abstract: Model-free reinforcement learning can acquire contact-rich robotic manipulation skills through trial-and-error interaction, but it often requires the policy to learn both task strategy and low-level motion generation. In this setting, the action representation is critical because it determines how policy outputs are converted into robot motion, shaping both exploration and physical execution. Direct Cartesian command interfaces require the policy to generate motion at every decision step, coupling task-level adaptation with continuous low-level control and increasing the learning burden. We propose PA-RL, a reinforcement-learning framework that uses artificial potential fields as the action representation. Instead of commanding motion directly, the policy adapts the parameters of an energy-like potential field, which generates a state-dependent guidance direction executed through a Cartesian impedance controller. We evaluate PA-RL on peg-in-hole insertion, a representative contact-rich task with nonlinear dynamics and discontinuous contact transitions. In simulation, PA-RL is compared with Cartesian velocity, Cartesian pose, and variable-impedance action spaces using the same RL algorithm. PA-RL is the only method to reach a 100% evaluation success rate within the allotted training time, while the best baseline reaches 92.6%. It also reduces joint-torque variation by 55.4% and Cartesian acceleration variation by 70.8% relative to the best baseline, without explicit motion-quality penalties in the reward. The simulation-trained policy further completes 9/9 real-robot insertions without fine-tuning, demonstrating the deployment feasibility of the learned potential-field interface.

</details>


### [138] [CounterPlay: Counterfactual Post-Training for Self-Play Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21617)
*Jiarong Wei,Yin Wu,Runkai He,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CounterPlay，一种反事实自博弈后训练方法，通过回溯失败任务并以不同驾驶风格重试，以极小成本显著提升驾驶策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高吞吐仿真中自博弈训练得到的驾驶策略闭环性能强，但随着训练规模扩大，单位仿真带来的提升递减。策略早期已学会应对常见场景，后续大量rollout反复遇到未解决的失败。后训练可针对这些失败，但现有方法主要在已访问状态评估备选动作或延续，而成功恢复可能需要更早地改变驾驶风格。

Method: CounterPlay包含三个关键组件：(1)失败驱动的回溯，利用策略的价值估计选择较早的存储状态重试任务；(2)奖励条件化，使单一策略能以从谨慎到激进的不同候选风格从该状态重试；(3)验证机制，仅当相对于事实分支没有其他车辆产生新的或更早的碰撞或偏离道路事件时，才保留完成任务的retry。通过验证的retry在全新随机性下被蒸馏进部署条件下的策略中。

Result: 在BehaviorBench上，CounterPlay仅用1B后训练transition（为anchor 100B自博弈训练预算的1%），在Interactive和Random两个split、全部八种交通场景下均取得state-of-the-art分数。改进在三种评估驾驶风格下均成立。CounterPlay解决了anchor在BehaviorBench上相当一部分超时案例，并实现了任务完成与安全之间的平衡，这是持续自博弈或更激进驾驶风格都无法达到的。

Conclusion: CounterPlay通过反事实回溯和风格条件化重试，以极低后训练成本有效解决自博弈训练中遗留的失败案例，在任务完成与安全间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Self-play in high-throughput simulators yields driving policies with robust closed-loop performance, but improvement per unit of simulation diminishes as training scales. Policies learn to handle common situations early, while further rollouts repeatedly encounter unresolved failures. Post-training offers an opportunity to target these failures, but existing methods primarily evaluate alternative actions or continuations at visited states, although successful recovery may require changing driving style earlier. We propose CounterPlay, a counterfactual self-play post-training approach that backtracks from failed tasks and retries them under alternate driving styles. CounterPlay rests on three key components. First, failure-driven backtracking uses the policy's value estimates to select an earlier stored state from which to retry the task. Second, reward conditioning enables a single policy to retry the task from this state using candidate styles ranging from cautious to aggressive. Third, CounterPlay retains task-completing retries only if no other vehicle incurs a new or earlier collision or off-road event relative to the factual branch. Retries that pass verification with fresh randomness are then distilled into the policy under its deployment condition. On BehaviorBench, CounterPlay achieves state-of-the-art scores on both the Interactive and Random splits across all eight traffic regimes using 1B post-training transitions, which is just 1% of the anchor's 100B self-play training budget. Improvements over the anchor hold across all three evaluated driving styles. CounterPlay resolves a substantial fraction of the anchor's timeout cases on BehaviorBench and achieves a balance between task completion and safety that neither continued self-play nor adopting a more aggressive driving style attains.

</details>


### [139] [Towards Fine-Grained Object Manipulation: SAM3-Guided Visuomotor Policy with Persistent Memory Learning and Focused Visual Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21621)
*Haolong Meng,Fangbo Qin,Mengchen Bai,Houwu Wang,Cirong Liu,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAM3引导的视觉运动框架，通过持久化物体记忆与聚焦视觉条件化，实现机器人对细粒度目标的精准操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度物体操作要求机器人从视觉相似物体中区分指定目标并可靠执行动作。但场景级视觉条件化缺乏显式目标选择，类别级引导又无法可靠区分同类别内的细粒度物体。

Method: 提出两个模块：1）FOM-SAM3，在SAM3完全冻结下，从有限多视角配准图像学习可复用细粒度物体记忆token，通过one-vs-rest学习编码持久记忆，用于定位目标并排除相似干扰物，可存入记忆库；2）FSAE，将物体内部局部外观特征与显式边界框坐标结合，为动作策略（DP、ACT）提供条件。

Result: 在包含四类粗类别共30个物理物体的FO-30数据集上验证FOM-SAM3；在三项真实机器人细粒度操作任务中，策略对干扰物表现鲁棒，能区分相似细粒度物体，并可扩展至新物体。

Conclusion: SAM3引导的框架通过持久物体记忆与聚焦视觉条件化，有效提升了机器人在干扰环境下的细粒度目标选择与操作能力，并具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Fine-grained object (FO) manipulation requires robots to distinguish a specified FO from visually similar objects and execute actions reliably despite scene distractors. However, scene-level visual conditioning lacks explicit object selection, while category-level guidance cannot reliably distinguish FOs within the same category. We present a SAM3-guided visuomotor framework that addresses these challenges through persistent object memory and focused visual conditioning. First, we introduce FO Memory-driven SAM3 (FOM-SAM3), which learns reusable FO memory tokens from limited multi-view registration images while keeping SAM3 fully frozen. Through one-vs-rest learning, these tokens encode persistent memories for localizing target FOs and rejecting similar alternatives, which can be stored in a memory bank. Second, we propose Focused Spatial-Appearance Encoding (FSAE), which combines in-FO local appearance features with explicit bounding-box coordinates to condition action policies including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT). The effectiveness of the proposed FOM-SAM3 was validated on the FO-30 dataset comprising 30 physical objects across four coarse categories. Across three real-robot FO manipulation tasks, our FOM-SAM3-guided policies demonstrated robustness against distractors, discrimination ability among similar FOs, and extendibility to new FOs.

</details>


### [140] [SynthDemo-RL: Breaking the Zero-Reward Barrier in VLA Adaptation with LLM-Guided Synthetic Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21650)
*Hiroaki Kingetsu,Hiroaki Kurihara,Kaoru Yokoo,Kenji Fukumizu,Manohar Kaul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SynthDemo-RL 教师-学生框架，用仿真特权状态自动合成成功轨迹替代人类演示，结合 SFT 与二元奖励 PPO 精调 VLA 模型，在无人类演示下显著提升 LIBERO-PRO、LIBERO 与 RoboTwin 2.0 的任务成功率与奖励覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型微调通常依赖人类遥操作演示，成本高；而稀疏二值奖励的强化学习在成功轨迹极少被采样时面临严重探索难题。论文旨在摆脱对人类演示的依赖，并解决稀疏奖励下的探索困境。

Method: SynthDemo-RL 采用教师-学生框架：自动化教师将仿真器特权状态转换为成功的操作轨迹；VLA 学生模型通过监督微调（SFT）从这些合成轨迹中蒸馏；随后用带二值任务成功奖励的 PPO 对学生进行精调。论文还引入"奖励覆盖率"（在固定评估协议下至少观测到一次成功的任务比例）作为平均成功率的补充指标。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 上，基于原始 LIBERO 微调的 pi_0.5 策略在 57 个计分任务中有 27 个成功率为 0%；直接用相同 PPO 配置与算力从该策略出发仅挽救 10 个任务，仍有 17 个为 0%。SynthDemo-RL 每任务仅用 50 条合成轨迹、无需新人类演示，挽救了全部 27 个任务，在 Position 与 Task 轴上平均成功率达 97.8% 与 97.1%。标准 LIBERO 上达到 96.0%，与使用每任务 50 条人类演示训练的 pi_0.5 相差仅 1.7 个百分点。在 RoboTwin 2.0 上进一步验证，且 MuJoCo 孪生中训练的策略轨迹可在物理机器人上开环执行。

Conclusion: 合成演示驱动的教师-学生流程配合 PPO 精调，能够在无需人类演示的条件下有效解决稀疏奖励探索问题，显著提升奖励覆盖率与成功率，并具备向真实机器人迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly relies on human teleoperation demonstrations, while reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse binary rewards faces an exploration challenge when successful trajectories are rarely sampled. We propose SynthDemo-RL, a teacher-student framework in which an automated teacher converts simulator-privileged state into successful manipulation trajectories, a VLA student is distilled from them by supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and PPO with binary task-success rewards refines the student. We study reward coverage, the fraction of tasks for which at least one success is observed under the fixed evaluation protocol, as a complement to the average success rate. On LIBERO-PRO, a public benchmark of perturbed LIBERO tasks for which no demonstrations exist, 27 of 57 scored tasks are at exactly 0% success for a pi_0.5 policy fine-tuned on the original LIBERO tasks. Direct PPO from this policy, under the same PPO recipe and the same RL compute as SynthDemo-RL's refinement stage, rescues 10 of these 27 tasks and leaves 17 at 0%. SynthDemo-RL, with 50 synthesized trajectories per task and no new human demonstrations, rescues all 27 and reaches average success rates of 97.8% and 97.1% on the Position and Task axes of LIBERO-PRO, respectively. On standard LIBERO, the same pipeline reaches 96.0% with no human demonstrations, within 1.7 points of pi_0.5 trained on 50 human demonstrations per task. We further validate the pipeline on RoboTwin 2.0 and verify that trajectories from a policy trained in a MuJoCo twin execute open-loop on a physical robot.

</details>


### [141] [Outcome-Conditioned End-Effector Geometry Across Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21659)
*Xingyu Lin,Zhuang Li,Zhongrun Wu,Shouquan Zhou,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过15000次LIBERO闭环rollout研究跨策略末端执行器几何：双成功策略对的几何差异显著小于单成功对，该次序在任务、策略对与多种表示下稳健，但幅度随表示变化；低跨策略距离并不意味可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略成功率相同并不代表物理执行一致；需要超越任务成功率，检验不同动作接口的VLA策略在闭环执行中的轨迹几何是否真正一致。

Method: 对4个策略、15,000次LIBERO闭环rollout中的末端执行器几何进行分析；主体采用3,600个配置匹配（因而相依）的策略对，用归一化动态时间规整距离度量，并在9种采样与带限表示、72步动作窗口、端点与时长调整及复合视觉压力下做稳健性检验。

Result: 双成功对的中位归一化DTW距离为0.0120 m，单成功对为0.0380 m；该次序在全部任务、全部策略对及9种表示下成立，但比值随表示变化数倍。双失败对差异更大但支撑薄弱，仅作探索性报告。成功执行中，替换伙伴带来的分离大于初始状态变化。匹配基线仍显示可测且异质的残余策略差异。成功执行与同任务示范的距离约等于示范彼此距离。复合视觉压力下策略排序与配对组成同时改变。

Conclusion: 跨策略几何差异的"双成功<单成功"方向稳健但幅度依表示而定，低跨策略距离不等于策略可互换；结果与任务相关几何兼容，但尚无法区分训练数据重叠与任务约束的贡献。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies solve the same manipulation task through different action interfaces, but task success alone does not establish whether their physical executions agree. We study cross-policy end-effector geometry in 15,000 closed-loop LIBERO rollouts from four policies. The primary clean-condition analysis forms 3,600 configuration-matched, and therefore dependent, policy pairs. Both-success pairs have a median normalized dynamic time warping distance of 0.0120 m versus 0.0380 m when exactly one policy succeeds.
